```text
Identify the Main Components of a Folloze Board
Now that you've had a glimpse of the platform, let's explore the ins and outs of a Folloze Board. Get ready to uncover its
main components, understand a range of use cases, and acquaint yourself with essential design tools.
We'll start with an overview of fundamental elements shaping your Folloze Board experience:
Terminology:
• Board: Content destination where you create and manage personalized, campaign-specific content and web page design
• Live Board: The active, customer-facing web page designed with Folloze, associated with a specific URL
• Template: Pre-created board design options provided by your Company Admin
• Header: Top section of a board, typically featuring your company logo
• Section: Horizontal portion of a board grouping related elements for specific purposes
• - Examples: column section, content section, testimonial section- Note: Folloze offers a variety of section types to meet your design requirements.
Additional details will be covered in the upcoming lesson.
• Footer: The bottom section of a board determined by your Company Admin, typically consistent across all boards.
```

```text
Board Templates
Determining a Board use case before getting started is crucial to setting the direction for content creation, layout, and
functionality. This early clarity ensures the Board serves its intended purpose, delivering a focused and effective visitor
experience aligned with specific goals and objectives.
The Company Admin will craft templates tailored to your use cases. When creating a new Board, choose the template that
aligns best with your goals to ensure best results and provide the maximum value to your end users.
Note: Click View Template for a preview of the
```

```text
Overview & Terminology
Understanding analytics and reporting is crucial for optimizing your Folloze experience. By delving into analytics, you gain
invaluable insights into how your Boards are performing, which content resonates with your audience, and where
improvements can be made. This data-driven approach empowers you to refine your content strategy, enhance user
engagement, and ultimately drive better results.
Before getting started, let's review some key terminology related to analytics and reporting:
Terminology
Campaign Types
• Inbound - link on a website, targeted ads, and social posts
• Outbound - marketing or sales email campaign, direct email through the Folloze platform, Outreach, and marketing automation
platforms such as Marketo
Folloze Identities - Accounts
• Known - domain is identified through an email address. The Board visitor landed from an outbound campaign with an email
address coded into the campaign link or filled out a form that included an email address
• Inferred - visitor's IP address or cookie information is associated with an account name and domain in Folloze or integrated
data enrichment services
• Unknown - does not have a known email in Folloze and no inferred data was returned from data services
Identity Types - People
• Guest - does not have a known email in Folloze and no inferred data was returned from data services
• Inferred unknown - account is inferred through data enrichment (see above)
• Unknown group link - visitor who lands on your Board using a trackable group link
• Known - has an email address and confirmed account (see known account above)
Note: Marketers often use the term "known traffic" when the traffic sources, as recorded by UTMs or other tracking parameters, are known.
Feel free to continue using this term while understanding the differences between it and the Folloze terminology.
Engagement Statuses
• Visitor - landed and spent time on the Board but did not click any content items
• Viewer - viewed 1-2 content items
• Super Viewer - viewed 3+ content items
• Clicker - clicked on a call to action button
1009.
CONTINUE >
```

```text
Overview & Terminology
```

```text
The Folloze Identity Model
Before diving into Board-level and account-level data, it is crucial to understand the different data outcomes that stem from
inbound campaigns (website, targeted ads, social posts) vs. outbound campaigns (marketing or sales emails). The Folloze
Identity Model defines how Board traffic is identified based on the received data.
```

```text
Create Reports in the Report Center
Creating reports in the Report Center is a vital aspect of leveraging data to inform decision-making and improve
performance within Folloze. Through the Report Center, you can generate comprehensive reports that provide insights into
various metrics, such as Board engagement, visitor activity, and content performance.
By harnessing the power of the Report Center, you can track progress, measure success, and continuously refine your
approach to achieve business objectives effectively.
For efficient reporting, save your filter values selection and re-use on demand.
Watch the video below to learn more about creating reports in the Report Center.
```

```text
Introduction
Let's dive into:
Folloze's ABX Blueprint
This course is divided into four main sections, one for each pillar of your next go-to-market (GTM) strategy.
1. Alignment: Establish account-based experience (ABX) GTM workflows
2. Experiences: Scale personalized buyer journeys
3. Engagement: Define precision GTM plays
4. Signals: Use data to drive impact
Each pillar has an associated template (spreadsheet) featuring real examples and best practices. All together they create
your ABX Blueprint! This course includes specific actions you can take with your team to align your people, processes, and
tools, resulting in a more effective and profitable GTM Team.
```

```text
Before You Get Started
To build a strong foundation for your GTM strategy, create a personalized engagement model at the top of your funnel using
a growth account-based marketing (Growth ABM) deployment model.
Note: If you're not familiar with Growth ABM, start with our Outperform Demand Generation Expectations with ABX Course, the first course in our ABX Masterclass.
Remember, each "funnel stage" is used by marketing and sales as a conversion management strategy. A strong team
strategy allows GTM Teams to convert accounts into customers with optimal performance and revenue results.
Our ABX Blueprint enables your GTM Team to provide the best buyer experience across every stage of the conversion
funnel; from top-of-funnel (TOFU), middle-of-funnel (MOFU), and bottom-of-funnel (BOFU) to customer adoption and
expansion.
In the video below, hear from Melinda Monaco, Senior Director of Growth Marketing at Folloze, as she shares:
• How TOFU fits into the overall funnel
• What you will takeaway from this course
• How our ABX Blueprint (spreadsheet) will help build your personalized engagement model
```

```text
The Four Pillars of a Strong ABX Blueprint
*
Building an ABX strategy from scratch can be complex. This course breaks it down into four essential pillars: team
alignment, buyer experiences, engagement, and data signals.
Below is a high-level overview of each pillar you'll use to build an effective ABX strategy for your organization. Click the "+"
to see more about each of the four pillars.
To effectively address organizational gaps, work with your GTM leadership to set up an ABX workshop. Regularly check
your goals every six months to boost engagement and growth. Feel free to tailor this course to fit your company's specific
terms and processes, so you can start and stay on track with ABX implementation.
Note: The Resources section of this course includes all downloadable templates, slides, and other resources from this course.
```

```text
The Center of Excellence (COE) Team includes marketing leadership members that are
responsible for transforming their engagement model from traditional demand generation to ABX (or at least something
more personalized).
This team must center the company vision for true buyer-centricity and work in partnership with marketing operations (MOPs)
to empower Experience Delivery Teams.
The Experience Delivery Team is comprised on frontline marketers, ADRs, and sellers.
This team requires technology, insights, and processes to engage buyers in highly personalized experiences. It empowers them
to prioritize activity based on 3rd-party intent and 1st-party engagement signals gathered from those experiences. This
ultimately optimizes pipeline performance and revenue.
```

```text
Terminology & Best Practices
>
Below you'll find key terms and best practices to keep your GTM Team and leadership aligned. Customize them to fit your
company's customer profile, sales process, and tools.
>
>
The "pre-pipeline account stage" identifies where an account is in their buyer journey based on intent and
engagement signals. It refers to accounts that have not entered the sales pipeline stage yet.
A "stage definition" explains an account's buying journey stage. While Folloze's definitions are provided,
it's crucial for your GTM Team to agree on and use consistent terms.
"Account volume" is the approximate percentage of total Ideal Customer Profile (ICP) accounts at this
stage.
O
"GTM Team involvement" refers to the marketing and / or sales team that owns the engagement,
movement, and conversion of a stage. Define clear ownership of each stage to optimize resources.
```

```text
Scale Personalized Buyer Journeys Align your team around the buyer by fostering a shared vision of buyer-centricity and encouraging them to think like the buyer. Your GTM Team should be able to efficiently and effectively meet the needs of your buyers through relevant, timely, and personalized experiences. In this exercise you will map buying committee personas and identify where, when, how, and with what content to engage them. Ask your GTM Team; what does it take to engage your buyers? How do they buy? What are their responses and objections? What is their preference between digital and human experience? How can you improve their experience? How do teams align, collaborate, and work together? What are the shared goals between your teams? Below, hear from Dee Anna Paredes, Co-Founder and Fractional Marketing & Strategy Lead at Infinite Edge Consulting, as she shares more about aligning around the buyer. 1:47
```

```text
anonymously. Intent data allows you find buyers early and influence their decision-making process

Ine rolloze top content items displays data about now people engage witn ine content items on rolloze boaras
allowing you to identify trends of an item across boards and see which items are most viewed.
0110
The "Folloze Intent.ai Topics" is a feature soon to be released. It presents a list of topics for a Folloze board, channel,
account, or other filters within the Folloze Impact Dashboard.
The "content type" refers to suggested content to be used as you craft your personalized buyer experience. It's
important to use content based on the buyer interest and intent as it shifts as buyers move along the buying journey
The "buying committee personas" refers to people on the buying committee that you need to engage through the
buying journey. As you move further into the funnel it's critical to engage all of the right people, especially "econic
decision makers" before they torpedo the deal.
The "personalization rule set" is a Folloze feature which is set up through a powerful rule-based engine that can
identify and update the Folloze board experience for Account Name, Contact First Name, and other attributes; as well
as offer specific assets relevant to the buyer. Personalization is easy to set up, scalable, increases engagement, and
differentiates you from your competitors.
```

```text
vision. Deliver relevant, timely, and personalized experiences to meet buyer needs. Orchestrate precision GTM plays to
target accounts based on volume, readiness, and stage goals. This allows for team investment into both cost-effective and

prospects.
```

```text
Use Data to Drive Impact
Buyer engagement is a critical and expanding area of focus for B2B marketers. Capturing B2B buyer signals across all
touchpoints by members of the buying team and at multidimensional levels, reflects true buying interest and is the most
impactful signal for marketers and sellers.
Third-party and first-party intent data offers marketers and sales teams the comprehensive insights needed to understand
every customer, drive effective team actions, and close more deals faster.
Hear from our team as they cover the importance of data and behavioral insights to predict outcomes.
```

```text
1. Request that your go-to-market leadership team watch the Folloze Transform to ABX Now Episode 1: Learn how to
effectively lead organizational change [45 minutes]
2. Download the free Folloze ABX Blueprint
3. Host an ABX workshop to review Folloze's ABX Blueprint, agree on definitions and goals, then plot the current and ideal
state of the GTM organization. It will help identify gaps in people, process, and technology requirements.
4. Inform marketing, ADRs, and sales teams of the new account-based GTM operating model by reviewing the ABX
Blueprint your leadership team built.
5. Bring the ABX Blueprint to life by building and executing your precision GTM plays.
6. Finish the last two courses in Folloze's ABX Masterclass
```

```text
What to Expect
Let's dive into:
Channel Tactics for ABX
Tactical teams should take this course to align Account-Based Experience (ABX) program goals with specific campaign
goals. This course is most valuable to Demand Generation Teams, Content Marketing Teams, Field Marketers, and
Account-Based Marketers (ABMers).
In this course, you'll optimize marketing channels across your top-of-funnel (TOFU). You'll use data-driven tactics and
automation for each part of the buyer journey (top, middle, and bottom of TOFU). This course equips you with tools to
create a cost-effective investment plan, use the most effective messaging and channels, and tactically improve your
campaigns.
For each part of the top-of-funnel, you will:
• Review the high-level needs of prospects specific to that part of TOFU
• Use messaging & content that aligns with their needs
• Focus your spending on the channels that best meet their needs
• Optimize channel-specific performance (engagement), with tactical benchmarks and example scenarios
Good marketing requires a healthy mix of channels and tactics.
Always aim to use a mixture of tactics that align well with your ABX program goals.
You'll achieve this by targeting accounts based on volume, readiness, and stage goals. This allows for team investment into
both cost-effective and highly personalized strategies. Because the needs of the buyer change as they progress through
the top-of-funnel, your strategy should also change to match their needs.
In order to simplify channel strategies, we've split TOFU buyer journey stages into three parts; top, middle, and bottom of
TOFU (shown below).
```

```text
Cost-Effective Brand Awareness
At the top of TOFU, your primary goal is to build brand awareness and engage a broad audience with minimal ad spend. In
the early stages of the buyer journey, you have an opportunity to introduce and position your brand to potential customers
and make a good first impression.
Focus on cost-effective channels such as display ads (DSPs), Google ads, and Linkedin, so you can reach a large audience
at a low cost. The key is to present compelling information that hooks your audience and encourages them to learn more
about your brand or industry.
```

```text
Messaging & Content for Top of TOFU
Top of TOFU content serves a critical purpose: to build category familiarity and brand awareness.
According to 6sense's 2024 Buyer Experience Report, B2B buyers are increasingly self-directed, with most of their buyer
journey occurring before their first contact with vendors. With this in mind, your messaging should focus on educating
prospects by illuminating the challenges and pain points they're experiencing.

The top of the funnel is your strategic testing ground for messaging, positioning, and design. With the broader audience at
this stage, you can experiment without risking high frequency. The key is to avoid sales-specific language or high-
commitment CTAs. Instead, use soft CTAs (ie. "Learn More" or "Explore") that spark curiosity and encourage lightweight
engagement. This is your chance to set the stage for deeper interactions in later TOFU stages.
Note: Keyword intent from Bombora, ABM platforms, paid search, and organic search will help you identify what topics your Ideal Customer Profile (ICP) is
engaging with and help guide content creation.
```

Structured Academy text:

```text
Messaging & Content for Top of TOFU Top of TOFU content serves a critical purpose: to build category familiarity and brand awareness. According to 6sense's 2024 Buyer Experience Report, B2B buyers are increasingly self-directed, with most of their buyer journey occurring before their first contact with vendors. With this in mind, your messaging should focus on educating prospects by illuminating the challenges and pain points they're experiencing. The goal is simple, make prospects feel understood, not sold to. You need to set the stage for future engagement by demonstrating industry knowledge and credibility through content that speaks directly to prospect challenges. For example, Folloze uses ad copy such as "What is a Buyer Experience Platform?" (purely educational) as well as “How to Personalize Content for the Most Effective Buyer Journey.” (addressing a pain point). Effective Top of TOFU content will: Illuminate challenges your prospects struggle with Contextualize solutions your brand provides Position your brand as a thought leader without selling Build credibility with educational content Example Top of TOFU content includes: Infographics: Visually represent common problems with clear and concise branded visuals. Snackable Videos: Produce short, engaging videos (1-2 minutes) that answer common questions. Industry Trends: Summarize key findings from annual reports, provide insights into emerging trends, and feature expert opinions to add credibility. Other Educational or Problem Definition Content: Address common issues and provide data or solutions in fun, digestible formats. The top of the funnel is your strategic testing ground for messaging, positioning, and design. With the broader audience at this stage, you can experiment without risking high frequency. The key is to avoid sales-specific language or high-commitment CTAs. Instead, use soft CTAs (ie. "Learn More" or "Explore") that spark curiosity and encourage lightweight engagement. This is your chance to set the stage for deeper interactions in later TOFU stages. Note: Keyword intent from Bombora, ABM platforms, paid search, and organic search will help you identify what topics your Ideal Customer Profile (ICP) is engaging with and help guide content creation.
```

```text
Table of Contents
List of activities in this course
Where to Invest for Top of TOFU
Now, we'll jump into each channel specifically.
INTRODUCTION
INCREASE BRAND AWARENESS FUK
Click through the interactive visual below or scroll down to read more about each channel highlighted for the top of
TOFU: Organic Web & Social, Paid Search, and Display Ads (DSPs).
DIFFERENTIATE FOR MIDDLE OF TOFU
SHOW PROOF FOR BOTTOM-TOFU
MICRO SEGMENTS
MEASURING CHANNEL SUCCESS
Organic Web & Social:
Optimize your site with relevant keywords, meta tags, and content to drive visibility through Google's search ecosystem. Google Search
Console provides insights into search behaviors, while platforms like Thruuu, SEMRush, and SEranking offer deeper competitive
intelligence.
Leverage social media platforms to build brand awareness. For B2B tech, we focus on Linkedin, Reddit, and Quora, along with industry-
specific platform communities. For Folloze, this includes ABM-centric Slack groups. Share educational content that creates connections,
not sales pressure
PROGRESS
- 100%
‹ BACK
CONTINUE >
• A Resource Center Board
• Embedded sections on your top performing site pages featuring
• Recently released content
• Upcoming events
• Thought leadership
Note: You can also consider targeted paid social ads to extend organic reach-just ensure they deliver measurable value at a low cost for your ideal custome
profile.
Paid Search (E.g. Google & Bing Ads):
Paid search offers low-cost impressions across your ICP, supporting organic marketing efforts. Target individuals actively searching for
solutions that you or your competitors provide.
Make sure to create compelling ad copy that aligns with your target audience's search queries. Use high-traffic, low-competition
keywords to maximize visibility and cost-effectiveness.
9 Example Destination: Your brand's website (ie. solutions page or home page)
Display Ads (DSPs):
Use programmatic advertising to manage display ad campaigns across multiple platforms. Aim to reach a broad audience that is close
to or just within your ICP. DSPs provide a centralized platform to run all of your ad campaigns across multiple channels depending on
where targeted users are at any given time. They are especially powerful for personalized, cross-channel retargeting.
With DSPs, you get access to a wide network of participating websites, and potentially better real-time pricing if you have high-volume
ad purchases. DSPs can also save you time managing campaigns across multiple ad management platforms or dealing with individual
publishers.
Again, try to target high-traffic, low-competition keywords and websites to build awareness. At this stage, you should focus on
compelling ad copy that introduces your brand and sparks interest. Regularly test ad creatives to identify what resonates
9 Example Destination: ABX Campaign Board
• Present curated Top of TOFU content (see messaging section for details)
Note: You can download channel-specific optimization guides in the Resources section of this course
POWERED BY NORTHPASS
```

```text
Top of TOFU Channel Optimization
*
At the top of the funnel, success isn't measured by immediate conversions or click-through rates. Instead, focus on metrics
that indicate growing awareness and interest across your target audience. Each channel offers unique metrics to gauge
brand lift and understand the influence of ads further down-funnel.
Map your ICP's content landscape by combining data from Folloze, buyer intent platforms (like Bombora), other ABM tools,
and search data. These data sources serve as a foundation that enables you to monitor which topics capture early-stage
attention and adjust your approach accordingly.
The slides below review benchmarks, metrics, and optimization tips for top of TOFU channels.
```

```text
Engage & Differentiate to Convert
Once prospects have moved beyond the initial brand awareness associated with top of TOFU, shift your focus to targeted
engagement and differentiation for the middle of TOFU.
>
According to sense's Buyer Experience Report, most buyers "select a preferred vendor before engaging sellers." Therefore,
your middle of TOFU content strategy is crucial... You must demonstrate expertise and build credibility during this
independent research phase.
Middle of TOFU channels generally require the highest campaign volume and ad spend since prospects are actively
researching solutions and comparing vendors. To effectively engage your audience at this stage, focus on Linkedin, DSPs,
and email marketing. These channels allow you to reach more specific audiences based on factors like industry, company
size, and job role.
```

```text
Messaging & Content for Middle of TOFU
Content for the middle of TOFU needs to educate and differentiate. Your messaging should demonstrate a deep
understanding of industry challenges while highlighting your brand's unique value proposition.
Buyer Journey Stages

Inem nere.
The goal is to demonstrate solutions and expertise while building brand credibility. Show prospects you do more than just
understand their challenges, you can solve them! Essentially, aim to validate your prospects' research process. You should
use a mixture of hard and soft CTAs at this stage.
For example, Folloze uses copy such as: "How Leading Companies Drive Pipeline with Personalized ABX" (solution-
focused). Plus, a mix CTA types like "Check it out" (soft CTA) and "Take a tour" (hard CTA).
Middle of TOFU Content Should:
PROGRESS
‹ BACK
CONTINUE >
• Showcase thought leadership and industry expertise
• Include social proof through customer stories and use cases
Persondilzauon iu.
Amplify Campaign
Effectiveness and
Pipeline Performance
with Dynamic
How to Guides, White Papers, Case Studies, Thought Leadership Webinars
Effective Middle of TOFU Content Includes:
• Solution overviews
• Videos: 2-3 minute demonstrations focusing on key benefits
• Click-through demos: self-guided tours that are <6 clicks
• Industry-specific case studies: Highlight measurable results and implementation success
• Interactive content: assessment tools or ROl calculators
• Direct comparison guides: help prospects easily evaluate 2+ solutions
Earlier in the top-of-funnel we focused on awareness of problems, at this stage, content should demonstrate a clear
understanding of industry challenges and present concrete solutions. This stage is your opportunity to showcase brand
value and differentiate. Prospects are actively researching solutions at this time in their journey, so your messaging can be
more specific and solution-oriented.
Gartner research shows that B2B buying groups spend only 17% of their time meeting with potential suppliers, making this
independent research phase crucial. You need to set the foundation for sales conversations by presenting solutions without
being overly promotional. The key is creating balance between educational content and solution positioning.
```

```text
Where to Invest for Middle of TOFU
Now that you understand messaging and content that resonates with prospects during this part of their journey, let's
explore what channels to invest in at this stage. Remember, middle of TOFU is where your content strategy can really shine
- it's your opportunity to stand out from competitors by delivering exactly what your prospects need, right when they need
it.
Click through the interactive visual below or scroll down to read more about each channel highlighted for the middle of
TOFU: Linkedin, DSPs, and Email.
Note: the next activity "When to Work with BDRS" covers Email Marketing & BDR Programs as a middle of TOFU channel.
< к°
Remember, generally your Board's default (aka unpersonalized version) should cater to the middle of TOFU buyer journey
stages.
PRUGKESS
• 100%
CONTINUE
Linkedin Advertising:
Linkedin can serve as one of your primary channels for this stage. It's effective because it allows for the precise targeting of
professional audiences that are actively researching solutions.
Consider structuring your ad investment for this channel into two campaigns that run simultaneously.
First Touch: Deploy medium-investment campaigns that feature document ads (ie. thought leadership like white papers, case studies or
ebooks). Or, use single-image or carousel ads that showcase industry insights or solution benefits. Use soft CTAs and target audiences
based on your top of TOFU engagement signals.
Note: First touches on Linkedin can stay on Linkedin as a destination.
Second Touch: Scale to higher-investment campaigns featuring video content and click-through demos leading to personalized Folloze
Boards that capture detailed engagement data.
9 Example Destination: ABX Campaign Board
• Customize content based on industry or role
• Feature customer testimonials relevant to the viewer
• Include self-guided product tours (4-6 clicks max)
• Provide comparison quides
• Enable direct meeting scheduling
Either way, you'll want to direct prospects to Folloze Boards with personalized messaging and content so you can track content
engagement and learn what is the most engaging to specific accounts and your audience overall.
Note: consider retargeting campaigns for engaging prospects that may have had a sudden drop in engagement.
Display Advertising (DSP):
Display ads are cheaper than Linkedin ads. But for mid-funnel DSP strategy, you need to target carefully. Aim for accounts that have
already shown interest in your type of solution. Use ads that show how your solution fits their needs.
9 Example Destination: Educational Content or Webinars
Consider sending prospects directly to bite sized educational content on Boards
```

```text
When to Work with BDRs
Your Business Development Representatives (BDRs) are most effective when they operate as Engagement Valets that
support buyers on their independent journey by helping them engage with the right content at the right time.
Best Practices for BDR Engagement:
• Timing matters: Connect with prospects after they've engaged with brand content
• Personalize outreach: Reference specific content they've viewed in your messages
• Multi-channel approach: Combine LinkedIn InMails, emails, and warm calls based on engagement patterns
• Be helpful, not pushy: Focus on providing additional value rather than pushing for meetings
Expected Outcomes:
• Increased Response Rates (compared to cold outreach)
• Accelerated Pipeline Development: faster progression from initial engagement to sales conversation
• Higher Content Consumption: More content engagement from accounts with personalized BDR follow-up
• Improved Meeting Quality: Better prepared prospects with specific questions related to their interests
Hear more from Kristi Tutt, Digital Marketing Specialist, about how to work with BDRs effectively in the video below or scroll to
see he details.
Best Practice: Respond to Signals

Note: Get additional insights about how BDR roles are being influenced by ABX from Helen O'Brien in this 4 minute clip of Folloze's 3rd Transform to ABX
Webinar.
Email Marketing / Outreach:
Mid-funnel email and outreach programs should evolve with your prospect's engagement signals. Build trigger-based
sequences that respond to specific engagement signals.
Key Engagement Signals to Monitor:
1. Folloze Board Signals
• Multiple Board link clicks from the same account
• Engagement time exceeding 2+ minutes on specific content
• Return visitors to the same Board within a 7-day period
• Multiple stakeholders from the same account engaging with content
2. Cross-Platform Signals
• Chatbot visits (e.g. Qualified) triggered by email clicks (e.g. Outreach or Marketo etc.)
• LinkedIn content engagement (comments, shares)
• Email response patterns (opened but not clicked, multiple opens)
• ABM platform intent signals showing active research
Effective BDR Trigger Plays:
When these signals appear, activate these proven plays!
1. Content-Based Outreach:
• Signal: Account spent significant time on specific content.
• Play: Share related, deeper content via personalized message.
• Example: "I noticed your team reviewed our case study on [topic]. Here's a more detailed guide that addresses the
specific challenges you might be facing."
2. Consideration Stage Account Engagement:
• Signal: Prospect has clicked links to Boards in auto-triggered emails multiple times, interacted with the chatbot (i.e.
Qualified) from an email click (i.e. Outreach), or appears in notifications due to being a consideration-stage account.
• Play: Have BDRs send Linkedin connection requests, personalized Sales Navigator InMails, warm calls, and one-off
emails with tailored content.
• Example: "I noticed you've been exploring our [specific content] recently. I thought you might find this additional
perspective valuable, as it addresses the [specific challenge] we're seeing in your industry."
```

```text
Middle of TOFU Channel Optimization
Effective middle of TOFU channel performance tracks campaign momentum. Folloze's Impact Dashboard highlights
Account Engagement Scores (shown below) at the organization-level; they're a great way to quickly assess momentum.
You can also use click-through rate (CTR) and other engagement benchmarks across channels to gauge momentum.
Account Engagement Scores O
Last updated: 8/11/2024,3:00:00 AM
Last Day
[All Active Accounts - filters do not apply]
Initial
Growing
Active
602
Surging
3,340 Accounts
During the middle of TOFU, you should sharpen your targeting by focusing on accounts showing stronger engagement
signals. Consider using industry-specific personalization so you can route engaged accounts to personalized Folloze
Boards with content tailored to their industry, persona, or problem. Focus on returning visitors and those spending more
time with your content. You can also launch re-marketing campaigns to re-engage visitors who've already shown interest in
your top-of-funnel content.
Build workflows that capitalize on growing engagement signals. When accounts demonstrate sustained interest through
multiple content interactions and/or Board visits, activate personalized sequences based on their consumption patterns.
Note: Keep your content fresh and relevant by maintaining a rotating selection of your top 5 content items for each industry, role, or use case. Update this mix every
4-6 months based on what's performing well. This approach ensures your prospects always see your most compelling content while keeping your workload
manageable
```

```text
Show Proof & Engage to get Buy-in
You're probably already familiar with traditional bottom-of-funnel (BOFU), which focuses on final conversion and purchase
decisions. However, for ABM programs, the bottom of TOFU represents the critical transition point where prospects move
from brand awareness and comparison to active solution consideration. This distinction is crucial for your ABM strategy.
Buyers typically choose their vendor before having their first conversation.
At the bottom of TOFU content must demonstrate value and facilitate direct engagement because buyers make decisions
prior to sales calls.
At the bottom of TOFU, your investment should shift to targeted, high-impact channels that drive meaningful engagement
with qualified accounts. This stage represents your highest cost-per-account investment, requiring strategic
personalization and precise targeting.
With the highest monetary investment per account, the bottom of TOFU content serves two critical purposes: to
demonstrate concrete value and to facilitate direct engagement. During this stage you can expect your main channels to
be Linkedin, and strategic email as well as your Sales Team(s).
Bottom of TOFU

Bottom of TOFU Quick Strategy Framework:
Demonstrate Value → Show concrete solution impact
Build Confidence → Provide comprehensive proof points
Personalize → Deliver account-specific content for 1:1 or 1:few campaigns, for 1:many, personalize experiences with
higher intent content and CTAs
Enable Action → Guide toward meaningful engagement
Note: This stage aligns with the Decision/Purchase [6s) and Engaged/MQA [DB] buyer journey stages, collectively known as the bottom of TOFU.
```

```text
Messaging & Content for Bottom of TOFU
Bottom of TOFU content serves two critical purposes: to demonstrate concrete value and facilitate direct engagement. At
this stage, your messaging should spotlight competitive differentiation while building confidence in your solution.
Buyer Journey Stages

CTAS

experience with clear CTs
You need to deliver key proof points that resonate with each audience segment while keeping the program scalable. Each
piece of content should advance prospects toward selecting your solution or considering it as a final contender. Success
here means tailoring proof points to your audience while staying flexible for program growth.
Bottom of TOFU Content Should:
• Showcase your competitive differentiation
• Build confidence with clear proof points
• Validate your solution's impact with customer evidence
• Guide prospects toward direct engagement
• Be personalized in an impactful way
PROGKESS
BACK
CONTINUE
Dive into our platform and most popular features
Product Demos, Customer Testimonials, ROI Calculators,
Competitor Comparison Content
Pricing pages, Interactive Tours, Product Webinars
Effective Bottom of TOFU Content Includes:
• Interactive product demos
• Competitive comparison guides
• Customer testimonials & success stories
• Industry-specific case studies with clear ROl metrics
• ROl calculators & value assessment tools
• Technical documentation featuring key capabilities
If you have the resources you can invest in personalized or 1:1 content at this stage. If you do not have the resources for 1:1
content, you'll need to prioritize personalized Board messaging and personalized content curation. Folloze believes in the
value and effectiveness of personalization especially at the bottom of TOFU. If you haven't already make sure to take
our Personalization for Growth ABM course.
Note: Keep personalization manageable by following the 80/20 rule. Build strong core content for most of your audience, then add custom
elements for your most important accounts. This lets you scale effectively while still giving special attention to your top prospects.
```

```text
Where to Invest for Bottom of TOFU
At the bottom of TOFU, your investment strategy should shift to targeted, high-impact channels that drive meaningful
engagement with qualified accounts.
Generally, you should prioritize Linkedin and personalized outreach programs since they offer the most precise targeting
capabilities. Support these primary channels with strategic email campaigns and personalized content.
Board personalization is extremely valuable at the bottom of TOFU. You can and should create custom experiences that
showcase your value proposition (at least for specific high-value accounts) at this stage.
Note: If you haven't already, complete Folloze's ABX Masterclass course; Personalization for Growth ABM which dives into the strategy and implementation of
personalization at scale.
You can also lean into your BDRs and Sales Team to take additional action at the bottom of TOFU. Integrations can even be
set up to send real-time notifications on Board engagement to flag when specific individuals are engaging
Click through the interactive visual below or scroll down to read more about each channel highlighted for the bottom of TOFU:
Linkedin, and Strategic Email & ADR Programs.
Note: the next activity "When to Work with Sales" covers Strategic Email & ADR Programs as a bottom of TOFU channel.
PROGRESS
• 100%
BACK
CONTINUE >
Linkedin Advertising
Linkedin offers precise targeting capabilities with proven impact. Brands advertising on Linkedin are generally viewed as higher quality
and more credible, leading to lifts in sales performance.
Start with first-touch campaigns featuring customer success stories, targeted InMail campaigns, and industry-specific solution
demonstrations. Progress to second-touch campaigns using conversation ads and account-specific messaging based on engagement
history. Gate high-value content behind Folloze boards to capture and track meaningful interactions.
9 Example Destination: ABX Campaign Board
• Customize content based on industry or role
• Feature customer testimonials relevant to the viewer
• Include self-guided product tours (4-6 clicks max)
• Provide comparison guides and ROl analysis
• Enable direct meeting scheduling
Note: This can be the same approach as what's used for the middle of TOFU but with more "Book a meeting" type CTAs (hard CTAs).
```

```text
Differentiate to Win
While buyer journey stages are a foundational aspect of effective ABM strategy, micro-segments like competitor intent
offer powerful opportunities for additional targeted engagement.
Other micro segments like lost opportunities, companies with compatible technology stacks, organizations experiencing
key events (like mergers or regulation changes), businesses showing growth signals (such as new funding or expansion),
or even current customers ready to expand can all run alongside your journey-based programs.
You don't need to invest aggressively into micro segments but it's advisable to select one or two that align with your ABM
program's goals.
In this section of the course, we briefly explore how to engage prospects actively comparing solutions or using competing
platforms. We'll focus on three key channels: paid search, Linkedin, and outreach programs.
Hear more about micro segment approaches from Kristi Tutt in the video below.
Loading...
PROGRECS
- 100%
‹ BACK
CONTINUE >
Build Recognition → Create category familiarity
Deepen Understanding → Showcase unique value proposition
Differentiate → Showcase unique value proposition
Nurture → Orchestrate precision GTM plays based on engagement
```

```text
Where to Invest for Competitor Intent
Strategic Paid Search
Lead your competitive investment strategy through targeted conquest campaigns. Bid on competitor brand terms,
comparison keywords, and "alternative to" searches. Monitor competitor metadata and top-ranking content to maintain
your competitive edge in search results.
9 Example Destination: A solutions or self guided tour page on your brand's website
• Embed (with Folloze's Web Engager) a content section that uses Content Al
Linkedin Advertising
Amplify your competitive differentiation through document ads featuring side-by-side comparisons, migration guides,
and success stories from companies that switched. Target employees at companies using competitor solutions,
focusing on renewal periods and event attendees for maximum impact.
9 Example Destination: Competitor/Comparison Board
• Create a dedicated space to highlight key differentiators and wins over competitors with:
° Demos
• Customer Success Stories
• Battle Cards (comparison charts)
• Integrations
Personalized Outreach Programs
Complete your competitive strategy with coordinated Sales Team efforts.
Deploy multi-touch sequences triggered by key moments like contract renewal periods or competitor product updates.
Support Sales Team efforts with competitive battle cards, comparison sheets, and success stories. Follow and receive
alerts in Sales Navigator (or similar tools) when key prospects in competitor accounts move over to new Tier 1 and Tier 2
accounts with targeted and personalized outreach.
Example Destination: A personalized Content Section on a Competitor/Comparison Board
```

```text
Micro Segment Channel Optimization
Quality & Delivery of content > Amount of content
Best Practices for Competitor Intent:
• Keep tabs on which differentiators get clicks, and what competitor content has the highest view time on your Boards.
• Refresh your comparison content regularly. Yesterday's customer story won't have the same impact as a fresh case
study.
• Consider competitor contract renewal periods; they're golden opportunities. Set up alerts so you can reach out at just
the right moment with your best differentiation content.
Best Practices for Micro Segments:
• Don't rush: Let your campaigns run for at least a month to gather real insights. During that time, keep your Google Ads
focused on those micro segment keywords.
• Set up smart triggers: When someone spends time with your micro segment content, follow up with relevant case
studies or guides.
• Stay agile: What messaging and content is actually moving the needle? What's getting real engagement? Use these
insights to refine your approach across all channels.
< BACK
CONTINUE >
```

Structured Academy text:

```text
Micro Segment Channel Optimization Best Practices for Competitor Intent: Keep tabs on which differentiators get clicks, and what competitor content has the highest view time on your Boards. Refresh your comparison content regularly. Yesterday's customer story won't have the same impact as a fresh case study. Consider competitor contract renewal periods; they're golden opportunities. Set up alerts so you can reach out at just the right moment with your best differentiation content. Best Practices for Micro Segments: Don't rush: Let your campaigns run for at least a month to gather real insights. During that time, keep your Google Ads focused on those micro segment keywords. Set up smart triggers: When someone spends time with your micro segment content, follow up with relevant case studies or guides. Stay agile: What messaging and content is actually moving the needle? What's getting real engagement? Use these insights to refine your approach across all channels.
```

```text
Overview
The role of the Company Admin in Folloze is crucial for ensuring effective management and configuration of the platform.
As the designated administrator, you have the authority to make critical decisions regarding Board settings, user
configurations, and integrations with other platforms.
This role empowers you to enhance security measures, personalize Board experiences, and streamline organizational
processes by assigning roles, managing users, and controlling access permissions.
Additionally, having access to comprehensive resources allows you to track important metrics and optimize the
performance of your organization's Boards effectively.
To enter the admin settings, click your profile image at the top-right corner and select Company Admin.
```

```text
Table of Contents

INTRODUCTION
GETTING STARTED
The Essentials
As a Company Admin on Folloze, here are the essential starting action items to consider. By addressing these key areas,
you lay a solid foundation for efficient Board management and maximize the platform's potential for your organization's
needs:
Note: Each link below will take you to a course with setup guidance. Please find additional links to help center articles for these topics at the conclusion
of this lesson.
RESOURCES
>
Getting Started Action Items:
• Custom Subdomain Setup: Create a custom subdomain for all your Folloze Boards to provide recognizable and
trusted URLs.
• Incorporate Company Branding: Get setup with company-approved branding, such as colors, fonts, images, and
videos for users to incorporate into their Boards, ensuring brand compliance.
• Create Board Templates: Standardize Boards by establishing design settings for specific use cases, streamlining the
content creation process and ensuring alignment with organizational goals and target audiences.
• Set Up Integrations: Connect Folloze with other essential platforms like CRM systems or marketing automation tools
to streamline workflows and data management.
• Customize Privacy Settings: Review and adjust privacy settings to align with company policies and compliance
requirements.
```

```text
If unsure where to begin, we suggest identifying individuals within your organization who can assist in completing these

integrations, while a Sales Manager helps with user additions and permissions.

structure and preferences.
```

```text
Table of Contents
List of activities in this course
Maintaining Privacy Compliance in Folloze
INTRODUCTION
COOKIE CONSENT MANAGEMENT
>
PRIVACY SETTINGS
RESOURCES
As a Folloze Company Admin, you must confidently and expertly navigate the complex landscape of data protection
regulations. Ensuring that your Folloze instance operates in accordance with regulations like GDPR, CCPA, and others is
critical in protecting buyer privacy and your organization's reputation.
Folloze provides you with all the elements needed to stay compliant. Typically, these 3 main functional areas that require
configuration:
Cookie consent management: You can choose to leverage Folloze's internal cookie policies and messages or integrate your
cookie consent manager, such as OneTrust, TrustArc, or another.
Forms - Opt-In/Out and Disclaimers: If you are leveraging Folloze Forms in your Boards, you can configure privacy
messages and opt in/out checkboxes to be reused on all forms. No action is needed if you are using marketing automation
forms.
Email Campaign Compliance: When incorporating links to Folloze Boards in your marketing automation or sales
development emails, those platforms will manage all the email compliance elements. Additional configuration in Folloze is
needed only if you are sending email campaigns directly out of Folloze, typically sales emails.
POWERED BY NORTHPASC
```

```text
Folloze First party Cookies Explained
>
>
>
The Folloze application makes use of various cookies to allow visitors to use the application and its features, to improve the
visitor experience, and to track visitors to Folloze Boards. While the majority of cookies used by Folloze are strictly
necessary, the lead tracking cookies are non-essential and may require a visitor's consent based on international privacy
regulations. By understanding cookies set by Folloze Boards, marketing and data privacy teams can maintain the requirec
compliances when deploying Folloze to an international audience.
Strictly Necessary Cookies

rotected board, the email_verification_code cookie is used

The email_verification _auth cookie is an optional cookie which
is set when accessing a protected board. When accessing a
protected board, the email_verification_auth cookie is used to
check if the visitor has authenticated into this board previously.
The after_auth_url cookie is an optional cookie which is set
when accessing a protected board. When accessing a
protected board, the after_auth_url cookie is used to redirect
the visitor to the appropriate URL after the visitor has
successfully authenticated.
The pending_auth cookie is an optional cookie which is set
when accessing a protected board. When accessing a
protected board, the pending_auth cookie is used to manage
the authentication process.
The password_auth cookie is an optional cookie which is set
when accessing a password protected board. When accessing
a password protected board, the password_auth cookie is
used to check if the visitor has authenticated with the
password into this board previously.
The folloze_cookie_match_pending cookie is used to indicate
that a board visitor is awaiting a potential identity change via
cookie matching. The folloze_cookie_match_pending cookie is
a temporary cookie and is removed by the system.

The folloze _lead cookie is used to track a visitor's identity.
The folloze_lead cookie allows for boards to be

folloze_lead is only set after the visitor has given consent.

The folloze_user_lead cookie is used to track and identify a 1 year
logged in Folloze user who has accessed the board,
typically for testing purposes. This allows a Folloze user to
be tracked as a known visitor rather than as a guest. The
folloze_user_lead is only set after the user has given
consent.
The showGreetingOnce cookie is an optional cookie which
is used to track whether a visitor has seen the board
greeting. The showGreetingOnce cookie is only set after
consent is given.
```

```text
Table of Contents
List of activities in this course
INTRODUCTION
Overview
Effectively organizing Board content is an important aspect for enhancing user experience and facilitating efficient
navigation. It not only enables you to easily locate relevant information, but also fosters a clearer presentation of content
and contributes to an engaging Board browsing experience.
CONTENT CATEGORIES &
SUBCATEGORIES
>
ADD, EDIT, & MANAGE CATEGORIES
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
>
Before You Get Started:
• Strategic Categorization:
• Categorize your Board content thoughtfully to enhance efficiency and streamline navigation. Remember that
Content Categories can feed wholly into Content Sections instead of selecting individual pieces of content.
• Don't Over-Do It:
• Most 1:Few and 1 :Many ABX Campaigns will benefit from minimal categorization and careful selection of content.
Many campaigns don't need more than 5-6 of your most relevant pieces of content.
• Optimize Display Order:
• Arrange categories and subcategories in a deliberate order to control the visual hierarchy of your Board
• Consistent Spatial Organization:
• Maintain a consistent spatial orientation (left to right or top to bottom) for a cohesive and user-friendly Board layout
POWERED BY NORTHPASS
PROGRESS

• 100%
CONTINUE >
```

```text
Activity: Categories & Subcategories
Your turn! Practice adding and editing categories and subcategories by following the steps below:
*
1. In the Content Tab of your Academy Test Board, add a new category called Featured.
2. Now create two more new categories and name them Category 2 and Category 3.
3. Under Category 2, create a new subcategory titled Subcategory 1.
4. Add a content item to each of the categories.
5. From the Home category of your Content Tab, drag and drop 3 content items into the Featured category.
6. Click into the Featured Category and drag and drop the content items to rearrange their order within the category.
Additional Steps: Keep adding additional pieces of content into your Board and arrange them into categories and subcategories. When
you're finished, head to the Designer to see how they appear on your Board!
```

```text
About Board Templates
Providing Folloze users in your company with well-crafted templates is not just a convenience but a strategic necessity.
These templates serve as the foundational building blocks upon which users construct their digital experiences. From
ensuring consistency in branding to streamlining the board building process, the importance of good templates cannot be
overstated. Here's why:
1. Consistency: Good templates ensure that all boards created within the company adhere to the same branding
guidelines, maintaining a consistent and compliant look and feel.
2. Scale and efficiency: Templates save users time by providing pre-designed structures and layouts, eliminating the need
to start from scratch. This accelerates the building process, allowing users to focus more on relevant messaging and
personalization rather than spending excessive time on design elements
3. Ease of Use: Well-crafted templates simplify the creation process for users, especially those with limited design skills
or technical expertise. Templates streamline the board building process and empower users to create professional-
looking boards with minimal effort.
Folloze provides you with several pre-built templates that follow best practices for various use cases. Demand gen landing
page and 1: ABM are just two examples of templates you will find in your instance. By default, branding elements from your
Theme Object will be applied to these templates.
We strongly recommend that you take the time with your frontline marketers and brand and design team to create a set of
templates that is visually appealing and customized to their most common use cases.
```

```text
Designing and Managing Headers & Footers
When designing templates, you can adjust or add any section. However, headers and footers are typically consistent
across multiple boards.
Headers are controlled through the designer, while footers are controlled through a combination of the designer and the
company admin.
Both headers and footers can also be created as custom html sections. This is useful when you want to mimic the
website's look and feel.
```

```text
INTRODUCTION
>
Overview
A content item refers to individual elements such as documents, videos, or articles that make up the content within a Board
These items are crucial as they help deliver targeted messages and engage audiences effectively.
This course will review how to add existing items in the Content Center to your Board as well as create new content directly
on your Board. Plus, get hands-on with effective content editing in your Content Tab.
UPLOAD NEW CONTENT DIRECTLY TO A
BOARD
ADD CONTENT FROM THE CONTENT
CENTER TO A BOARD
>
EDIT EXISTING CONTENT IN A BOARD'S
CONTENT TAB
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
Before You Get Started:
• Select content thoughtfully
• Most Top-of-Funnel ABX Campaigns require no more than 5-6 of your most relevant pieces of content. As your
buyers make progress in their buying journey, offer additional content.
• Align content with goals
• Keep your content item's copy and visuals aligned with your Board use case and business goals. For example,
prioritize short form and video content for TOFU campaigns. Also consider optimizing content items for load time.
This helps keep content items engaging
• Check the Content Center first
• Verify that the content you want is not available in the Content Center before adding it directly to Boards. This helps
avoid duplicate content and keeps analytics accurate.
```

```text
Activity: Upload New Content
Your turn! Let's practice adding new content to your Academy Test Board by following the steps below:
*
Download file
1. Click Download file to download an example PDF
2. From the Board's Content Tab, click Add Content
3. Select the type of content you'd like to add; in this case the PDF File you just downloaded
- Note: The maximum file size is 25MB for images and 1GB for videos.
4. A modal will appear for you to configure the content item's settings
• Details: customize the title, vanity URL, description, and thumbnail
• Tags: add keywords to help categorize your content items
• Visibility: Set gating preferences on your item and/or schedule when the content item will appear on your Board
• Metadata & SEO: configure search engine settings for that specific content item
- Note: SEO can be turned on/off via the Board Settings Tab.
- Note: SEO may not be appropriate for your ABX Campaigns. Turn SEO off if, for example, this is a 1:1 ABX Board targeted at a specific account
5. Repeat steps 2-4 but this time add a Web Link like www.folloze.com but for your company
6. Repeat steps 2-4 again but this time click add an Article
• Add a Title to your article like [Test Article]
• Click Publish in the top right of your Article
- Note: You should have 3 items now; one PDF File, one Web Link (URL) and one Article.
```

```text
>
Edit Existing Content Items
After you've added items to your Board, you can adjust and edit them from the Content Tab by simply clicking on the piece
of content. Using the content editor modal that appears, you can update item thumbnails, modify their titles, descriptions,
and vanity URLs for your specific Board use case. Plus set items online or offline, and more.
Practice editing existing content and using quick actions in the next activity!
Tip: Generally, if you need to update an item's content, such as its link, file, or article, you'll want to do that via the Content Center so it is updated across all Boards
using that asset.
Note: Titles and thumbnail images are not propagated to the Boards when changed in the Content Center, only the content itself.
>
```

```text
INTRODUCTION

CONTENT TAB
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
1. Edit a Content Item: click on a content item to open the content editor
2. Update Details: modify the content item's title, description, vanity URL, or settings
3. Customize Thumbnail: click the pencil icon on the top right corner of the current thumbnail image to crop it or click
Upload File to upload a new thumbnail image
4. Update a Content Item: click Browse in the file field or enter a new link in the URL field to update your content source
- Note: to change the file or web link for a content item added from the Content Center, you must edit it in the Content Center or click Detach to disconnect it
from the original. Detaching it creates a new unique content item.
5. When you're done, click Save in the bottom right corner
6. To quickly duplicate, share, view, or set an item offline; hover over the item from the Content Tab and use the quick
action icons (starting from left to right):
• Click the Copy Item icon to duplicate the item
• Click the Share Item icon to get a link to the item
• Click the Set Offline icon to quickly set the item offline
• Click the Eye icon to view the item in a new tab
```

```text
Table of Contents
```

```text
INTRODUCTION
ADDING A CONTENT SECTION TO Al
BOARD
Overview
Now that you've organized your content into categories, it's time to showcase it on your Board.
As you've learned, a Content Section is a designated area within a Board that is used to display large blocks of content
items. Content Sections streamline the browsing experience for viewers, making it easier to access and digest the
information presented on the Board, while also allowing users to highlight specific assets or categories to suit their
objectives.
EDIT A CONTENT SECTION
>
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
Before You Get Started
• Multiple Content Sections:
• Enhance the versatility of your Board by incorporating multiple content sections, allowing for diverse presentation
• Strategic Placement:
• Optimize viewer engagement by strategically placing content sections. Consider featuring essential content near
the top and organizing a comprehensive resource hub towards the bottom.
• Stacked Sections:
• Foster coherence in your Board layout by stacking two content sections. For instance, pair a content-with-tabs
section with a single video section for seamless navigation.
POWEREn BY NORTHPASC
PROGRESS
```

```text
Add a Content Section
There is a variety of Content Section format options. Some of the most common include:
• Content with Tabs: Display content items divided into category tabs
• Content Spread Out: Display all content items in a scrollable section with filter and search options
• Single Item: Select a single leading item to display in the Content section or choose to dynamically display the most
popular item
Watch the video below to learn how to add a Content Section and view different formatting options:
```

```text
Overview
When you integrate Folloze with other tools in your MarTech stack, you maximize efficiency, effectiveness, and ultimately,
your bottom line. Some of the benefits of the integration include:
Delivering Personalized Experiences at Scale: Use account and contact attributes from your ABM platform and Marketing
Automation Platform to personalize Folloze experiences to your target accounts and contacts in real-time
Enrich Your Tracking and Attribution Models: The rich engagement analytics coming from Folloze will enhance your
tracking, attribution, and scoring models.
Trigger Automated Buyer Journey Flows: Identify moments that matter and trigger additional touch points throughout the
buyer journey
*
Let's take a look at the available integrations:
Marketing Automation Platforms (MAPs):
• Marketo
• Eloqua
• Hubspot
• Pardot
Customer Relationship Management (CRMs):
• Salesforce
• Dynamics (please contact support)
ABM Platforms (Data Providers):
• 6sense
• Demandbase
• D&B RevUp (please contact support)
Tracking, ChatBots, & Other Scripts
• Google Analytics
• Adobe Analytics
• Drift
• Qualified
• And more...
CONTINUE >
```

### MAPs Integrations

#### Page 002 - Marketo

```text
Marketo
The integration between Folloze and Marketo has multiple benefits:
• The ability to connect and sync activities from Folloze into Marketo for downstream processing, including lead scoring
and routing
• Marketo forms can be embedded directly into Folloze Boards, allowing for centralized form creation and submission
tracking from Folloze to Marketo.
• Munchkin cookies facilitate tracking of various activities such as page views and form submissions, enhancing data
collection for smarter marketing decisions. In parallel, Folloze retrieves known lead attributes and can leverage them
for personalization and enrichment of the experience.
• Identity exchange between the Munchkin and Folloze cookies enhances both platforms' identity models and enriches
lead information
Integrating Marketo Forms into Folloze Boards - A Deep Dive
The Folloze & Marketo Form integration offers users a powerful and flexible way to collect lead details when using Marketo
Forms embedded on Folloze Boards. Using Marketo embedded forms with Marketo's Lead Tracking Munchkin script allows
for greater functionality with the ability for hidden fields such as UTM codes to be populated via URL query parameters
cookie values, or referrer parameters. For the greatest functionality and flexibility, pair Marketo embedded forms and the
Marketo Munchkin with the Marketo Forms 2.0 API and Folloze Board custom attributes. Using all three in tandem,
Marketers and Marketing Operations teams can streamline the process of form creation and attribution and enrich forms
with custom data for downstream lead processing.
Use Cases:
Marketo Form Attribution: Marketo Form attribution allows marketing teams to create a single form that can be used across
multiple boards. For example, marketers can create a simple contact form collecting first name, last name, and email while
also collecting program or campaign specific data in hidden fields populated by Folloze Board custom attributes. By doing
so, each form fill, regardless of board, can be easily attributed to the appropriate program or campaign in Marketo.
Marketo Downstream Lead Processing: Which business unit did that board belong to again? This is one of many examples
of how form enrichment can help downstream lead processing. With Marketo's powerful automation capabilities and
Folloze's flexibility, additional, hidden information, stored in Folloze Board custom attributes, can be passed through a form
submission to allow marketers to be more effective in processing leads. In the example above, by specifying the business
units at the board level. marketers can ensure that form submissions from boards correspondina to a specific business unit
are routed to the appropriate teams.
Click to view the course below, which provides necessary steps and documentation for setting up Marketo.
PROGRESS —
How To: Marketo
```

```text
Hubspot
HubSpot's marketing automation capabilities allow for seamless activity tracking of prospect and customer engagement
across various marketing channels. By syncing form submissions, rich analytics, and engagement data captured by Folloze
to HubSpot, marketers can effectively engage clients, report on attribution, and develop ongoing marketing strategies.
Leveraging HubSpot's Custom Behavioral Events (CBEs), marketers can gain insights into prospect interactions with Folloze
content, enabling targeted reporting and workflow automation to optimize conversion and engagement strategies.
```

```text
Pardot
Folloze integrates with Pardot through a custom script, facilitating the connection between Folloze Forms and Pardot Form
Handlers. This integration captures visitor identity for Board personalization and tracks behavioral analytics on accounts
and prospects.
Form submissions are synced to Pardot for marketing automation and lead management purposes, enabling various use
cases such as demo requests, content gating, or registration.
Additionally, by adding a Salesforce Pardot Tracking ID, Folloze page views can be tracked in Pardot, submitting page
events like Board and item views as webpage views.
*
```

```text
INTRODUCTION
MAPS INTEGRATIONS
CRMS INTEGRATIONS
ABM PLATFORMS (DATA PROVIDERS)
Salesforce
The integration between Folloze and Salesforce offers significant benefits to both marketers and salespeople.
Most Folloze Admins opt to integrate Folloze with their Marketing Automation Platform, with the engagement data flowing
from the MAP to Salesforce. This ensures data integrity and prevents duplications, providing a cohesive view of customer
interactions across platforms. However, for some customers, especially Pardot users, integrating Folloze directly with
Salesforce offers a more comprehensive perspective of buyer engagement, extending beyond the form submissions
enabled by the script.
For salespeople, the ability to embed the Folloze Account Engagement Dashboard directly into the account view within
Salesforce is invaluable. This integration places engagement data right at their fingertips, eliminating the need to switch
between tools to monitor their accounts' activity.
Based on your MarTech stack, use case, and Folloze licenses, you can choose to install only one, both, or none of the
options above.
TRACKING, CHATBOTS, AND OTHER
```

```text
SCRIPTS
Delivering personalized, timely, and relevant buyer experiences across a wide range of digital touchpoints has become table
stakes for revenue teams. Yet with all the investments we've made, it can still seem like our channels, tools, and teams are
disconnected which makes it difficult to create a seamless experience for an individual buyer, much less their whole team.
In today's dynamic market, B2B buyers are increasingly designing their own journeys, choosing a wide variety of information
and perspectives before they are willing to engage with sales. For marketers, this opens up the whole field - the entire
buying cycle is now ours. That means for every account, contact and situation, there must be the ability to dynamically
sense where the customer is and what they need, tailor the message to the journey stage and context, and leverage
engagement insights as signals to deliver quality opportunities and pipeline as inexpensively and quickly as possible.
The integration between Folloze and platforms like 6sense, Demandbase, and D&b RevUp empowers marketing and sales
teams to engage buyers on their unique buying journey and help them quickly discover and engage with what is most
relevant to them. This powerful approach not only builds buyer preference, but turns your entire GTM team into an efficient,
effective, pipeline machine.
The integration itself is simple yet powerful, as you can see in the following course.
```

```text
Why Add Scripts?
Scripts are commonly used in various ways when added to Folloze Boards, extending functionality, enhancing user
experience, and enabling interaction. Some common ways in which Folloze customers use scripts include:
Traffic Tracking and Analytics: Scripts such as Google Analytics, Adobe Analytics, or similar services are embedded into
boards to track visitor traffic, behavior, and other metrics. The data generated from those tracking applications is then
aggregated to give marketers a complete picture of traffic and engagement across their various web assets.
Chatbots and Live Chat: Scripts are often used to integrate chatbots or live chat functionalities like Drift, Qualified, and
others, allowing companies to engage with buyers in real-time. Folloze can track the engagement with the ChatBot and
report it back to trigger the bot playbook.
Compliance: As discussed in the Privacy course, many Folloze customers use Cookie Consent Managers, added as custom
scripts to the Folloze boards.
Tag Managers
Many Folloze customers opt to manage scripts by adding their tag manager script, like Google Tag Manager or Tealium, to
all boards. This offers a centralized, easy way to deploy scripts to Folloze Boards.
```

```text
Types of Scripts & Best Practices
Types of Custom Scripts:
Organization-level Script: Applies the script to all boards within your organization. Example: Implement a Google Tag Manager (GT)
script across all organization boards.
Board-level Script: Targets an individual board with a specific script. Example: Implement a script that displays a 3rd party interactive
graphic that is only needed for a single board
Best Practices When Deploying Scripts:
Follow these best practices when adding scripts to Folloze boards for a successful deployment:
Testing on Test Boards: Always test custom scripts on a dedicated test board before deploying them to live environments
to identify and resolve potential issues
Script Isolation: Test each script individually and in combination with others, as scripts may interact and impact each
other's functionality
Descriptive Titles: Assign clear titles to scripts for easy identification in the browser console, facilitating effective
troubleshooting and management
Multi-Browser and Device Testing: Ensure scripts work seamlessly across different browsers and devices, including
popular choices like Chrome, Safari, and at least one mobile device, to guarantee widespread compatibility
Tag Management Platform: If leveraging tag manager platforms such as Google Tag Manager or Tealium, deploy all scripts
from your tag manager to consolidate script management
```

```text
Overview
At Folloze, we understand the importance of maintaining a cohesive brand identity across your digital properties. Brand
compliance was at the top of our minds when we designed the platform. We wanted to ensure that Frontline Marketers can
create amazing digital experiences in a highly scalable way while staying within the brand guidelines.
Brand elements in Folloze, namely colors, fonts, and buttons, are centrally saved and managed in the Theme Object. The
Theme object is a collection of preset elements that are automatically applied to every board. If a company rebrands,
updating the theme object will automatically propagate the change to the company boards.
Folloze will utilize your company's website and brand guidelines to create the Theme Object for you, ensuring minimal lift
on your end!
To get started, simply send your brand guidelines to your CSM or email support@folloze.com. If you have a
custom font, please include the font files as well.
Best Practices
• Folloze Boards echo your brand but are different from your website
• Remember that the experience on a Folloze is often intentionally different
than the experience of a website visit. When you design buyer journeys, the
different touch points are cohesive but not identical
• Primary Color and Gradients:
• Provide one primary color and, if applicable, gradient shades, including
lighter and darker shades of the primary color for flexibility in design.
Note: The primary color will act as your main button color. The darker and lighter shades will be used
for hover effects.
• Maintain Consistency:
• Ensure that fonts, colors, and logos used align with your company's
established brand guidelines to maintain a cohesive identity.
```

```text
Add Logos to your Media Library
As mentioned in the previous video clip, at a minimum, we recommend uploading your approved brand logos. This ensures
that your logo will be a good resolution and adhere to your brand guidelines regardless of the Board it is used on.
Like all assets uploaded to the Media Library, uploading logos increases team efficiency by limiting the need to download
and upload assets while building Boards.
Note: Adhere to your brand guidelines. If your brand only uses the full color version of your logo, do no upload a black or white version.

RESOURCES

2. Click Upload and at a minimum, please upload:
• Your brand logo in color or black with a transparent background
• Your brand logo in white with a transparent background
3. Toggle on Activate Logos
Your Logos are now easily accessible from all Board Designers!
Note: For logo files, PNG or SVG file formats are preferred.
```

```text
In less than 30 minutes, design a rich and
engaging experience to support any use case

departments in order to update online campaigns and deliver personalization at scale.
```

```text
Overview
After selecting the suitable template, building your Board involves crafting layouts, selecting images, choosing colors, and
organizing content to communicate information and engage the audience effectively. Good Board design drives desired
actions such as engagement with content, clicks, or conversions, while also enhancing user experience, conveying
messages clearly, and capturing attention.
In this course, you will create a Board titled "Academy Test Board" to serve as a practical canvas for lesson activities
moving forward.
Note: You will need a Creator License to create Boards and have a hands-on experience. If you don't have one, please contact your Company Admin.
Before You Get Started
• Strategic Experience Planning:
• Begin by strategically outlining the key messages, visuals, and content to ensure a cohesive and impactful
Board. Ask yourself:
• What is the goal of my campaign?
• Is the messaging aligned with the goal and with the buying stage?
• Is all the content relevant to my buyers?
• Is the CTA appropriate for the channel and stage?
• Visual Appeal Matters:
• An on-brand, visually cohesive digital experience will result in more engagement
• Stay on-brand by using templates, images from the Image Gallery, and pre-defined brand colors
• Use your board to tell a story. Leverage the different sections to create visual interest.
• Effective Board Details:
• When filling in Board details, provide a clear and concise internal name, relevant tags for easy searching (using
'#' to separate tags), and a description optimized for SEO, when applicable. While the description is not
displayed, it enhances discoverability.
```

```text
*
Your turn! Create a new Board in Folloze by following these steps:
1. Login to Folloze
2. Click the +Add Board button at the top right to create a new board
- Result: The Create New Board modal appears
3. Select any desired template
- Click View Template to preview a template
4. Click Next > at the bottom right of the modal to create a new board based on the selected template
5. Complete the board details
6. Only the board name is mandatory for this course, title your board Academy Test Board
7. Add the tag Academy, a brief description, and include an image for internal identification
8. Toggle off Set as company board to make the board private
- Note: Each new board is visible in the Company Directory by default
```

```text
1. While in the Designer, locate the top section you updated in the previous activity.

technology.
- Best Practice: Opt for a background image that is simple rather than complex - it will help your text stand out!
3. Adjust the color of your text to contrast the background color.
4. Adjust the Padding Top to M and the Padding Bottom to S.
5. Preview the Board.
6. When you finish making edits, click Publish in the top right corner.
Additional steps: Continue to gain hands-on experience by repeating the process for different sections, and exploring various customization
possibilities.
```

```text
Manage a Section
Great work!
As you'll discover, all section types use the same Section Editor toolbar and background editing options. Each section also
has unique customization options, found in the Manage Section pencil icon tool. Additional edits can be made to the
Board's inline elements, such as text, buttons, and forms, which can be effortlessly added within your section.
Note: External forms, like those from Marketo or Eloqua, and button colors/shapes and fonts are configured and added in the Company Admin settings.
Now it's time to customize Board sections and action buttons. The video below will detail editing options that are specific
to a few frequently-used sections as well as button editing options.
```

```text
Table of Contents
List of activities in this course
INTRODUCTION
>
SHARE A BOARD WITH A LINK
>
Overview
Trey, are you ready to launch your board?! It looks great, it's personalized, and it offers relevant content. Now is the time to
create meaningful engagement!
The most common way to launch a Folloze ABX campaign is to configure a link to the board and share it through various
digital channels. Inbound, like paid or organic social and targeted ad campaigns, or outbound like marketing and sales
emails.
In this course we focus on configuring a sharable link and optimizing for a personalized experience that is fully trackable.
SEND A BOARD WITH FOLLOZE +
OUTREACH API
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
Before You Get Started
• Prepare your tracking parameters:
• Whether you're using UTMs or other tracking parameters, make sure you have those parameter values available to
you as you configure the link. By default, Folloze uses the common UTM scheme for tracking traffic sources, but
you, as the user, are responsible for appending the proper UTM values to the links.
• Distinguish Link Types:
• Clearly understand the distinctions between different link types (Public Link, Campaign Link, Individual Trackable
Link, Group Trackable Link) to tailor your sharing strategy. Match each link type for its specific use case, enhancing
the effectiveness of your outreach efforts.
```
