Tell Compelling Data Stories
*
In B2B marketing, the gap between data and decisions is bridged by storytelling.
Your metrics carry powerful insights about market dynamics, buyer behavior, and strategic execution - but only if you frame
them within a meaningful narrative. Instead of presenting isolated data points, weave them into a story that reveals
patterns, validates strategic choices, and demonstrates marketing's role in business growth.
When done effectively, this approach transforms marketing dashboards from static reports into dynamic tools that drive
alignment and inspire action.
Craft stakeholder-specific narratives about your impact metrics and drive action with Melinda Monaco in the video below.

alignment. A well-structured feedback loop transforms your ABX program into a dynamic, evolving strategy that responds
to market changes and business needs.

GTM Team Alignment:
• Unifies sales, marketing, and success teams around what's working
• Creates a common language around pipeline and revenue impact
• Enables quick strategic pivots based on performance data
Demonstrating Marketing Impact:

as stories with three parts can create excitement and get results.

grew

Key Success Factors
7 Key Factors for ABX Success
Implementation success comes from starting simple,
maintaining consistency, and gradually building
sophistication as your program matures.
1. Executive sponsorship
2. Cross-functional alignment
3. Clear ownership
4. Regular communication
5. Continuous learning
6. Data-driven decisions
7. Agile improvement
Remember, your ABX measurement journey is ongoing. Start with strong fundamentals, tell compelling stories with your
data, and create feedback loops that drive continuous improvement. Success comes from balancing ambition with
execution, always keeping your eye on the metrics that matter most to your business.

Table of Contents
List of activities in this course
INTRODUCTION
Overview
Email campaigns in Folloze offer a streamlined approach to reaching your audience with personalized messages. By
harnessing the power of email within your Folloze account, you can create, send, and manage campaigns with ease,
ensuring optimal engagement and tracking.
*
NEW EMAIL CAMPAIGNS
>
CRAFT YOUR EMAIL
SEND AN EMAIL CAMPAIGN WITH
FOLLOZE
>
SEND & REVIEW A CAMPAIGN
Before You Get Started
• Sender Selection Strategy:
• Choose the appropriate sender type (Me, Someone Else, or Multiple Senders) for your email campaign based on
your objectives.
• Audience Precision:
• When crafting an email, prioritize adding accurate audience details, including first name, last name, and email
address. A precise audience ensures personalized communication and accurate data collection, enhancing the
impact of your email campaigns.
• Timely and Scheduled Delivery:
• Optimize the delivery of your campaigns by choosing between immediate sending or scheduling them for a future
date and time. This allows strategic planning, aligning with your target audience's availability for increased
engagement.

Create a New Campaign
Run and manage email campaigns directly within your Folloze account. Emails can be sent under your name or on behalf

Me - send a campaign under your name and out of your inbox
Someone else - send a campaign on behalf of someone else from your organization, email will be sent to this individual for approval
before sending
Multiple Senders - send a campaign on behalf of multiple senders from your organization, it will be sent to these individuals for their

Table of Contents

Activity: Create & Test a New Campaign
Your turn! Practice creating a campaign then crafting a test send by following the steps below:
INTRODUCTION
NEW EMAIL CAMPAIGNS
CRAFT YOUR EMAIL
SEND AN EMAIL CAMPAIGN WITH
FOLLOZE
>
SEND & REVIEW A CAMPAIGN
>
1. While in the Content tab of your Academy Test Board, click Create Campaign.
2. Name your campaign Academy Test Campaign.
3. Select Me as the sender.
4. Click the Audience tab.
5. Click Contacts and add your first name and email address as the email recipient.
6. After your contact is added to the column on the right, click the pencil icon to edit the contact.
7. Edit the contact by add a last name and company.
8. Click update then click save.
9. Click the Message tab.
10. Click Content link in the top right corner of the default message and insert the link to the board.
11. Click Insert field in the top right corner of the default message and insert the recipient first name merge field into the
salutation of your email.
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
>
Note: When using dynamic merge fields, do not adjust the bracketed text, as it is needed to insert the correct information into your
message.

Overview
A custom subdomain in Folloze reinforces brand identity and creates a seamless browsing experience for users, instilling
trust and credibility in your platform.
Having a custom subdomain is also important for tracking traffic and engagement across all your web properties and for
managing first-party cookies.
Lastly, if you wish to leverage our Website Engager and embed a Folloze board or section of a board within a page on your
website, the Folloze board has to be on the same root domain.
Here are some popular custom subdomain examples:
• engage.yourcompany.com
• explore.yourcompany.com
• experience.yourcompany.com
• discover.yourcompany.com

Table of Contents

INTRODUCTION
CUSTOM SUBDOMAIN
404 PAGE
RESOURCES
- Back to My Courses
Set Up a Custom Subdomain
If your company has already completed the steps to set up the subdomain, there is no need to do this activity.
Take the first steps by following the instructions below:
Steps:
1. Determine the subdomain you wish to use for all Boards
2. Add the following sets in your DNS settings:
- Create a CNAME (Host Alias) for the chosen subdomain
- Point the new CNAME to the host: v3gateway.folloze.com
3. Email support@folloze.com to let them know which subdomain you have chosen and that you have pointed that
subdomain to Folloze.
- Folloze Support will take it from there and will let you know once the process is complete!
Post Activity: Check to confirm the subdomain has been properly set up. You can use an online DNS checker to ensure the CNAME has
been pointed to v3gateway.folloze.com.
*Note: If your company requires using a Third-Party certification, please contact support@folloze.com for alternative steps.
POWERED BY NORTHPASC
PROGRESS

to v3gateway.folloze.com. After you point the CNAME, you do not need to take any additional action.

NAVIGATING THE ABX
TRANSFORMATION
Watch the webinar
In today's ever-evolving business landscape, the shift towards Account-Based
Experience (ABX)-driven pipeline generation is no longer an option; it's a necessity.
Join us for an enlightening webinar where we delve into the transformative journey
that hundreds of companies have undertaken, guided by industry leaders, Folloze and
Infinite Edge Consulting.
Discover the secrets to success as we explore real-world case studies showcasing
how this paradigm shift has not only become a game-changer but a must for B2B
marketing leaders. Those who've embraced ABX strategies are already witnessing
extraordinary results, with campaign outcomes skyrocketing by 5 times or more, and
substantial savings of hundreds of thousands of dollars on marketing campaigns.
But here's the catch: transitioning to an ABX-driven pipeline generation model
involves a comprehensive overhaul. It's not just a change in strategy or marketing
approach; it's a transformation that reverberates throughout your organization,
touching upon your relationship with sales and customer success teams.
In this webinar, you'll gain invaluable insights into the 'how' behind this transformative
process. Learn the practical steps, best practices, and strategies to not only embrace
ABX but also bring your entire organization on board. Join us for this illuminating
session, and let Folloze and Infinite Edge Consulting guide you towards mastering
ABX-driven pipeline generation, ensuring your business thrives in the modern market
environment.
Don't miss out on this opportunity to stay ahead of the curve. Watch On Demand.

When the economic landscape becomes a question mark, some marketers see disaster and others see opportunity.
The willingness to rise to a challenge is one thing. But being well positioned to seize the moment in today's chaotic

buyer-centric environment. Practically speaking, that means the ability to take real-time advantage of data that gives
you the richest picture of buyer engagement and experiences - so you can continuously adapt your approach and
ensure you meet them with exactly what they need to keep moving forward.

- Back to My Courses
Table of Contents
List of activities in this course
INTRODUCTION
Overview & Terminology
Board settings are non-design elements like the Board's URL, metadata, etc. It's important to configure the Board settings
in alignment with the use case, as we'll see in this course.
Before reviewing Board settings in detail, let's start by understanding some key terminology:
*
GENERAL BOARD SETTINGS
ACCESS & SECURITY SETTINGS |
>
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
>
Terminology
• Settings Tab: {ộ} (Cog Icon) Where you configure general settings that are related to building a Board
• Board Metadata: Data associated with your Board that is displayed when you share a link on social media platforms.
• Localization: Way to quickly adapt parts of an existing Board to the local language of the target market.
• Favicon: Custom image (usually a png of your company logo) that displays on your live Board's browser tab.
• Editor: A licensed Folloze user (usually a marketer), who can edit the Board design and configuration.
• Subscriber: A licensed Folloze user (usually a salesperson), who is associated with a specific Board. Subscribers can
view but not edit the Board.
• Inviter: A licensed Folloze user (usually a salesperson) who is set to invite buyers to the board. The Inviter determines
multiple attributes, as you will see in the course. The Inviter must be an editor or subscriber on that specific Board
• Owner: A licensed Folloze user (usually a marketer), who owns a specific Board. By default, the user who created the
Board is its owner.
POWERED BY NORTHPASC
PROGRESS
-
• 100%
CONTINUE >

Trey, it's your turn! Update the general Board settings by following the steps below in your Academy Test Board:
*
INTRODUCTION
GENERAL BOARD SETTINGS
>
ACCESS & SECURITY SETTINGS
>
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING
1. Click the Settings tab on your Board.
2. Set your Board vanity URL to "Academy-Test-Board".
3. Enter a display name for your Board to set your Board's title metadata.
4. Click Select Image to upload/set your Board's image metadata.
5. Enable Personalization on your Board.
6. Click Save to finish updating your settings.

Activity: Board Access & Security Settings
Your turn! Update your Academy Test Board access and security settings by following the steps below:
*
INTRODUCTION
GENERAL BOARD SETTINGS
ACCESS & SECURITY SETTINGS
>
1. In your Board Settings, click Access and Security.
2. Click 'By Password' to set and require a password for accessing your Board.
3. Enter the password "Academy" for your Board.
4. Click 'Save'.
CHECK YOUR UNDERSTANDING|
>
Tips:
• Remember, gating by password protection or 2FA can increase bounce rates and is not be a good fit for 1:Few and 1 :Many digital campaigns.
• SSO must be implemented by your Company Admin and Folloze Support. If you currently use SSO to log into Folloze, then it is already configured and
you can use SSO for gating.
